style=\"max-width: 100%;\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\n\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOpenClaw is an AI assistant that runs on your devices and meets you in the channels you already use. It connects models, tools, messaging channels, and optional companion apps through one Gateway, for a single operator or for a team whose members trust each other: the same gateway runs as a personal assistant on one laptop or as a shared \u003ca href=\"https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/teams\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eteam deployment\u003c/a\u003e, and configuration is the only difference. class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OpenClaw 🦞 — Your assistant, on your devices, in your chats</h1><a id="user-content-openclaw---your-assistant-on-your-devices-in-your-chats" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenClaw 🦞 — Your assistant, on your devices, in your chats" href="#openclaw---your-assistant-on-your-devices-in-your-chats"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 <p align="center" dir="auto">

</p>
<p dir="auto">OpenClaw is an AI assistant that runs on your devices and meets you in the channels you already use. It connects models, tools, messaging channels, and optional companion apps through one Gateway, for a single operator or for a team whose members trust each other: the same gateway runs as a personal assistant on one laptop or as a shared <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/teams" rel="nofollow">team deployment</a>, and configuration is the only difference. The architecture case — trusted gateway, untrusted execution, deterministic policy — is in <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/why-openclaw" rel="nofollow">Why OpenClaw</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://openclaw.ai" rel="nofollow">Website</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai" rel="nofollow">Docs</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/getting-started" rel="nofollow">Getting started</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/why-openclaw" rel="nofollow">Why OpenClaw</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/showcase" rel="nofollow">Showcase</a> · <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/help/faq" rel="nofollow">FAQ</a> · <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/VISION.md">Vision</a> · <a href="https://deepwiki.com/openclaw/openclaw" rel="nofollow">DeepWiki</a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The installer supports macOS, Linux, and Windows. It provisions a supported Node.js runtime when needed.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# macOS / Linux / WSL2
curl -fsSL https://openclaw.ai/install.sh | bash"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> macOS / Linux / WSL2</span>
curl -fsSL https://openclaw.ai/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> bash</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Windows PowerShell
iwr -useb https://openclaw.ai/install.ps1 | iex"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Windows PowerShell</span>
iwr <span class="pl-k">-</span>useb https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>openclaw.ai<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Already manage Node.js? Install the published package instead (Node 22.22.3+, 24.15+, or 25.9+):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g openclaw@latest --allow-scripts=openclaw"><pre>npm install -g openclaw@latest --allow-scripts=openclaw</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">That command is for npm 12 or npm 11.16+. On npm 11.15 and earlier, omit
<code>--allow-scripts=openclaw</code>. See the
<a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/install" rel="nofollow">installation guide</a> for the lifecycle script
contract, Docker, Nix, and other deployment paths.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">On a fresh install, the installer scripts start onboarding automatically.
Complete the wizard they open. If you installed the package directly with npm,
pnpm, or Bun, run:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="openclaw onboard --install-daemon"><pre>openclaw onboard --install-daemon</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">After onboarding:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="openclaw gateway status
openclaw dashboard"><pre>openclaw gateway status
openclaw dashboard</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Onboarding verifies model access, creates the workspace, and configures the Gateway. The last command opens the Control UI; send a message there to confirm the assistant is working. See the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/getting-started" rel="nofollow">getting started guide</a> for channel setup and troubleshooting.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How it fits together</h2><a id="user-content-how-it-fits-together" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How it fits together" href="#how-it-fits-together"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway" rel="nofollow">Gateway</a> is the local control plane for sessions, tools, events, and channel connections.</li>
<li>The <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui" rel="nofollow">Control UI</a>, CLI, and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/tui" rel="nofollow">TUI</a> connect to the Gateway.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels" rel="nofollow">Channels</a> bring the assistant to WhatsApp, Telegram, Slack, Discord, Google Chat, Signal, iMessage, and other messaging services.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms" rel="nofollow">Companion apps and nodes</a> add voice, Canvas, camera, screen, and device-local actions on supported platforms.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">OpenClaw works with hosted and local <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-providers" rel="nofollow">model providers</a>. Its <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools" rel="nofollow">tools</a>, <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills" rel="nofollow">skills</a>, and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins" rel="nofollow">plugins</a> extend what an assistant can do.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security</h2><a id="user-content-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security" href="#security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Treat inbound messages as untrusted input. DM-capable channels pair unknown senders by default; approve a pairing request with <code>openclaw pairing approve &lt;channel&gt; &lt;code&gt;</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Tools run on the host for the main session unless you configure sandboxing. Read the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security" rel="nofollow">security guide</a>, <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/exposure-runbook" rel="nofollow">exposure runbook</a>, and <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/sandboxing" rel="nofollow">sandboxing guide</a> before connecting other users or exposing the Gateway remotely.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h2><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development</h2><a id="user-content-development" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development" href="#development"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The repository is a pnpm workspace. Plain <code>npm install</code> at the repository root is not supported.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git
cd openclaw
pnpm install
pnpm build
pnpm ui:build"><pre>git clone https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> openclaw
pnpm install
pnpm build
pnpm ui:build</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a> for the contribution workflow and the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/setup" rel="nofollow">source setup guide</a> for the development loop.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Community</h2><a id="user-content-community" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Community" href="#community"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">OpenClaw is developed in the open by the <a href="https://openclaw.org" rel="nofollow">OpenClaw Foundation</a>, a non-profit. See <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/CONTRIBUTING.md">CONTRIBUTING.md</a> for maintainers and contribution guidelines; AI-assisted PRs are welcome.</p>
<p dir="auto">Use the <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/new/choose">issue chooser</a> for bugs and feature requests, ask setup questions in <a href="https://discord.gg/clawd" rel="nofollow">Discord</a>, and report vulnerabilities through <a href="/openclaw/openclaw/blob/main/SECURITY.md">SECURITY.md</a>. New capabilities usually belong in plugins built on the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/building-plugins" rel="nofollow">plugin SDK</a> and shared through <a href="https://clawhub.ai" rel="nofollow">ClawHub</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">OpenClaw was built for <strong>Molty</strong>, a space lobster AI assistant, by Peter Steinberger and the community. Explore the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/start/lore" rel="nofollow">project lore</a>, <a href="https://soul.md" rel="nofollow">soul.md</a>, <a href="https://steipete.me" rel="nofollow">Peter's site</a>, <a href="https://www.star-history.com/#openclaw/openclaw&amp;type=date&amp;legend=top-left" rel="nofollow">Star History</a>, and <a href="https://x.com/openclaw" rel="nofollow">@openclaw</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Special thanks to <a href="https://mariozechner.at/" rel="nofollow">Mario Zechner</a> for his support and for <a href="https://github.com/earendil-works/pi">pi</a>, and to Adam Doppelt for the lobster.bot domain.</p>
